# 1149 new apps — 2026-11-16

Window: apps first found between 2026-11-15 18:09 UTC (19:09 Europe/Warsaw) and 2026-11-16 19:04 UTC (20:04 Europe/Warsaw) and released since 2026-11-14 18:09 UTC (19:09 Europe/Warsaw), plus apps found earlier whose release date arrived in that span. Date-only release dates count by calendar day in America/Los_Angeles. Storefronts: us.

## Coverage

- **Warning:** Apple answered errors to 10 batches in a row (IDs 6,834,876,870 – 6,834,878,869) while the canary apps still came back, so the run kept going; failed IDs are listed under failed batches.
- **Warning:** Apple's response format changed: missing fields price.
